# Overlap feature inventory before removal

Baseline `6b50f447fcc5be467e9314d7c602b688755957b9`; branch `cx-overlapaxe`, after the requested fetch/merge.
This inventory was committed before production or test edits. Every matching tracked text
reference (including gzip receipts) is indexed by original file and line in
[references-before.txt.gz](references-before.txt.gz); the table below lists every file/line.
`IoPipe`/`io_pipe`/`iopipe` are included because much of the feature does not spell “overlap”.
The baseline is newer than the context's original 344-row gate.

## Verified feature and removal boundaries

- `src/core/config.h:354-358,840-845,1151-1153,1240-1244`: boot-only `uint32_t overlap = 0`, 0|1 parser/help/validation and accessor. Replace storage with reserved bytes to keep Config 624 and all surviving offsets. `--x-overlap` and `--thread-pipeline` are already unknown, not active aliases.
- `src/cmd/t_server.cc:338-339,2103-2110,2155-2158`: CONFIG registration supplies GET, immutable SET and REWRITE; INFO `overlap`, `overlap_enabled`, and schedule witnesses. Removing registration yields ordinary unknown CONFIG behavior.
- `src/core/io_loop.h:409-435,505-748,902-1287,2277-2555,3063-4060,4190-4690,5354-5360,5528-5564`: boot specialization, deferred parser/TLS/CQE dispatch, depth gate, IFID/WB stage scratch, prefetch and interleaved/natural split schedules. Trace helper references before deleting: fused IFID uses shared geometry and parsing helpers too.
- `src/core/rl2s.cc:75-94`: overlap choice also arms split IO with read-local; retain its Pipeline-0 path and all reader functionality.
- `src/core/reorder.cc:67,728-982,1296-2066,2067-2180,3068-3130`: executor prefetch caller, mirrored R7 IO helper templates and schedule plumbing. Preserve fused R7 and default split routing.
- `src/core/ex_loop.h:2230-2256,2305-2307`: KEEP flattened owner-batch prefetch for eligible fused batches, including read-local=0/reorder=0; remove split opt-in and overlap witness only. Retain singleton, ownership, scatter/cursor/random and exact-slowlog guards. Ordinary `prefetch_exec_batch` also survives.
- `src/core/orthog.h:25-87`, `src/core/server.h:402`: optional schedule sidecar and overlap counters/schedule enum/INFO formatting. Retain reorder accounting, 64-byte stride and surviving offsets; allocate only for reorder.
- `src/core/iopipe_pipeline.h`: split experiment's geometry, depth gate, loop state and compile-time rotation. Delete obsolete machinery; retain any constants needed by the fused baseline at their surviving owner.
- `src/core/boot_support.h:26-41`, `src/core/genthread.cc`, `src/core/genthread_pipeline.h`, `src/core/wb_rule.h`: banner and comments, plus writeback helper dependencies.
- `docs/CONFIGURATION.md:50`, `tomokv.conf:117-121`: knob documentation (“2s owner prefetch and IO overlap; eligible 1s owner batches prefetch regardless”), remove row/block and regenerate with `tests/docs_drift.py --write`.
- `read_local.h`, `read_local_parse.inc`, `tracking.cc`, `atomics_glue.inc`, `flatstore_atomic.inc`: inspected; matches are key conflicts, TRACKING prefix checks or MVCC version overlap, not this feature. Do not change.

## Gate/test/harness plan

`tests/gate.sh` currently expects quick **504**, full **521**. The feature matrix is
collected at lines 3359-3376, BEFORE the quick exit at 3407. Its overlap dimension
must go, while every split correctness scenario survives with the default schedule.
Split-overlap writeback witnesses must be removed or redirected only where their
sole purpose is the deleted schedule. Final exact row delta will be counted from
collection lines and committed with EXPECT updates, as explicitly authorized by
step 3 of this task (overriding the shared rule against lane count edits).

`feature_gate.py`, `gates_test.py`, `orthog.py`, `mode_equivalence.py`, `knobs.py`,
`config_parser_test.cc`, INFO/boot fixtures, overlap-prefetch and reorder-engagement
fixtures, source-shape tests, and all booting harnesses in the index require review.
`tests/overlap.py` also contains surviving read-local correctness: preserve it under
an appropriate name/grammar. `tests/abbagate.py` currently permits omitted unknown
OFF knobs on the reference; replace that inference with a named refusal. Historical
binary compatibility must explicitly select equivalent retired-off behavior rather
than silently discard any requested flag. The x-overlap translation is harness-only.

Keep the `ov=0` recipe field: it is part of the shared cell/receipt schema and the
protected campaign imports that parser. Convert active recipes, not recorded past
results or archived source/patch artifacts. [cells-before.json](cells-before.json)
records per-file recipe counts. The generic `/tmp/claude-1000/generic_merit_cells.txt`
has 14 fused cells, all ov=1; read it only, copy with ov=0. Add four 2s GET/SET p32/p8
cells for the requested 18-cell null.

## Campaign exclusion

`tools/mkprobe_probe.py:262` emits `--overlap`; line 476 checks its INFO echo.
**Left for the campaign; remove after it.** Do not edit that tool or
`build/mkprobe-mainline`. It freezes `tests/headline_cells.txt` by SHA-256 and compares
`tests/mkprobe_cells.txt` with headline plus `tests/wb_rule_cells.txt` (lines 127-140).
This conflicts with changing every active cell file; the requested snapshot or
recipe exclusion must be explicit, and any native self-test failure must be reported.
The staged PRE-axe binary is `/home/user/Projects/cx-final/build/mkprobe-mainline/server/tomokv`.
